2026-11-12T09:51:52.8607053Z [WebServer] - External changing data without sending a snapshot of it along with the HTML.
2026-11-12T09:51:52.8607698Z [WebServer] - Invalid HTML tag nesting.
2026-11-12T09:51:52.8608076Z [WebServer] 
2026-11-12T09:51:52.8638028Z [WebServer] It can also happen if the client has a browser extension installed which messes with the HTML before React loaded.
